# Government reform

Please help with verifying or updating older sections of this article.
At least some were last verified for [version](/Europa_Universalis_5_Wiki%3AVersioning "Europa Universalis 5 Wiki:Versioning") 1.0.

*This article is about government reforms; for other meanings see [Reform (disambiguation)](/Reform_%28disambiguation%29 "Reform (disambiguation)").*

Representing everything from ancient traditions to progressive amendments, reforms outline the shape of governance in a [Country](/Country "Country"). Each one is unique, and they often give powerful trade-offs or open up unique play styles. Every country starts with only one slot for a reform, but can increase it with certain [Advances](/Advances "Advances"). Reforms which are specific to a government type can only be selected by countries which have that type and if they are obtained in another way they are lost (on the next month tick) if the country has the wrong government type.

Adding a reform is free but removing a reform costs 20 [![Stability.png](/thumb.php?f=Stability.png&width=24)](/Stability "Stability") Stability. If the primary religion is [![Sānjiào](/thumb.php?f=Religion_sanjiao.png&width=24)](/List_of_religions#Sānjiào "Sānjiào") Sānjiào it also costs 10 [![Righteousness.png](/thumb.php?f=Righteousness.png&width=24)](/Righteousness "Righteousness") Righteousness.

Some reforms may be **locked** and cannot be removed manually until certain conditions are met or no longer active.

| [![Kanrei Council icon](/images/3/33/Government_reform_shogunate_kanrei_council.png)](/File%3AGovernment_reform_shogunate_kanrei_council.png "Kanrei Council icon") Kanrei Council  Seeking to form a more cohesive relationship between the Shōgun and Shugo Daimyō of Japan, a council of the many Kanrei allows Shugo Daimyō council members to advise the Shōgun while also engaging with other Shugo Daimyō council members. | [![Legacy of Osman icon](/images/0/0a/Government_reform_legacy_of_osman.png)](/File%3AGovernment_reform_legacy_of_osman.png "Legacy of Osman icon") Legacy of Osman  The Legacy of Osman is a tradition of divine right to expand under the central religious and political authority of our supreme leader. We invoke the Legacy to justify our continued conquests, maintain dynastic legitimacy across diverse holdings, and create an empire where our conquered peoples are not merely subjugated, but fully absorbed. | [![Ruling Commission icon](/images/4/42/Government_reform_ruling_commission_of_england.png)](/File%3AGovernment_reform_ruling_commission_of_england.png "Ruling Commission icon") Ruling Commission  A commission of Nobility has effectively taken over the management of the kingdom, acting as regents. | [![Council of Ten icon](/images/6/69/Government_reform_council_of_ten.png)](/File%3AGovernment_reform_council_of_ten.png "Council of Ten icon") Council of Ten  The Council of Ten is the major governing body of the Venetian republic, focused on the election of the Doge as well as checking the power of the patricians. | [![Kilwan Trade Communities icon](/images/5/54/Government_reform_kilwan_trade_communities.png)](/File%3AGovernment_reform_kilwan_trade_communities.png "Kilwan Trade Communities icon") Kilwan Trade Communities  A series of loose confederations, often led by a governor that strengthens the power of local merchants, these communities push for local autonomy and urbanization. Their trading skills provide a steady stream of revenue for our treasuries. | * [![Monthly towards plutocracy.png](/thumb.php?f=Monthly_towards_plutocracy.png&width=32)](/File%3AMonthly_towards_plutocracy.png) +0.10 Monthly Progress to Plutocracy * [![Global burghers estate power.png](/thumb.php?f=Global_burghers_estate_power.png&width=32)](/File%3AGlobal_burghers_estate_power.png) **+50.00%** Burghers Power * [![Global merchant capacity modifier.png](/thumb.php?f=Global_merchant_capacity_modifier.png&width=32)](/File%3AGlobal_merchant_capacity_modifier.png) **+20.00%** Trade Capacity * [![Monthly towards free trade.png](/thumb.php?f=Monthly_towards_free_trade.png&width=32)](/File%3AMonthly_towards_free_trade.png) +0.10 Monthly Progress to Free Trade |  | None | 2 years | [![Flag of Kilwa](/thumb.php?f=Flag_ZAN.png&width=24)](/Kilwa "Kilwa") [Kilwa](/index.php?title=Kilwa&action=edit&redlink=1 "Kilwa (page does not exist)") |
| [![Control of the Mahdali Coinage icon](/images/5/54/Government_reform_control_of_the_mahdali_coinage_reform.png)](/File%3AGovernment_reform_control_of_the_mahdali_coinage_reform.png "Control of the Mahdali Coinage icon") Control of the Mahdali Coinage  The might of our dynasty is imprinted upon the coins that circulate the markets from the Italy coast, to India, and beyond. This trimetallic coin is primarily made of Copper, Silver, and Gold, and is the first of its kind to exert such influence on a multiregional scale. The compartmentalization and clear assignment of tasks to each makes for a more effective administration and serves as an extension of the ruler's authority. | * [![Country cabinet efficiency.png](/thumb.php?f=Country_cabinet_efficiency.png&width=32)](/File%3ACountry_cabinet_efficiency.png) **+10.00%** Efficiency of Our Cabinet * [![Global crown estate power.png](/thumb.php?f=Global_crown_estate_power.png&width=32)](/File%3AGlobal_crown_estate_power.png) **+10.00%** Crown Power |  | Allowed for upgrades during the *"[Ruler's Name]'s Reforms"* event | 2 years | [![Flag of Ayutthaya](/thumb.php?f=Flag_AYU.png&width=24)](/Ayutthaya "Ayutthaya") [Ayutthaya](/index.php?title=Ayutthaya&action=edit&redlink=1 "Ayutthaya (page does not exist)") |
| [![Malian Tribute System icon](/images/8/8c/Government_reform_malian_tribute_system.png)](/File%3AGovernment_reform_malian_tribute_system.png "Malian Tribute System icon") Malian Tribute System  Our primary source of Gold, the Malian Tribute System is a system which demands that all Gold is taxed and ultimately controlled by the ruler. | * [![Subject pays tributary cost modifier.png](/thumb.php?f=Subject_pays_tributary_cost_modifier.png&width=32)](/File%3ASubject_pays_tributary_cost_modifier.png) Allow Tributary Subjects: **Yes** * [![Subject pays vassal cost modifier.png](/thumb.php?f=Subject_pays_vassal_cost_modifier.png&width=32)](/File%3ASubject_pays_vassal_cost_modifier.png) **+30.00%** Income from Vassal Subjects * [![Government size.png](/thumb.php?f=Government_size.png&width=32)](/File%3AGovernment_size.png) **+1** Cabinet Seats * [![Monthly towards decentralization.png](/thumb.php?f=Monthly_towards_decentralization.png&width=32)](/File%3AMonthly_towards_decentralization.png) +0.05 Monthly Progress to Decentralization |  | Demanded tributes during the *"Restoring the Tributes"* event | 4 years |

| [![Local Audiences icon](/images/8/89/Government_reform_eth_wandering_court.png)](/File%3AGovernment_reform_eth_wandering_court.png "Local Audiences icon") Local Audiences  Rather than having a stationary court, we prefer the freedom to move at will to other parts of the nation. | [![Mandala System icon](/images/6/6b/Government_reform_mandala_system.png)](/File%3AGovernment_reform_mandala_system.png "Mandala System icon") Mandala System  The Mandala is a system of power in which independent city states swear fealty to whoever leads the most powerful state among them.
